| DEFER-14 | modify/delete 冲突的呈现方式（git 不写冲突标记、保留 HEAD 版本并输出 `CONFLICT (modify/delete)`；Libra 写标记） | 属冲突呈现轴，不在标签轴内；2026-09-15 实测发现 | 用例移植或用户需求 | 待立 issue |
| DEFER-15 | `compat_release_homebrew_verify::homebrew_verification_rejects_command_and_identity_failures` 在本机稳定失败（`tap-path unexpectedly succeeded`） | 与本计划写集无关的发布校验 shell 夹具：`FAIL_STEP=tap-path` 的场景在本机（macOS，真实 `brew` 在 `/opt/homebrew/bin`）未按预期非零退出；HF-31 的 T-1 全量首次暴露，证据 `/Volumes/Data/tmp/issue477/hf31/homebrew-verify-preexisting.txt` | 收口门前在计划基线复现并定位夹具与宿主 bash 的差异 | 待立 issue（夹具或宿主环境修复） |
| DEFER-16 | 三个 pkt-line 网络用例在全量下 45 s 超时（`ls_remote` 两例、`push` 一例） | 上游 `7c43a87`/`6062118` 引入的本地 HTTP 服务器用例对负载与时序敏感：全量下 3/3 重试均超时，单跑时 `empty_response` 与 `push` 仍失败、`https_marker` 通过；与本卡写集无关，证据 `/Volumes/Data/tmp/issue477/hf31/pktline-timeouts-preexisting.txt` | 收口门前在计划基线复现，判定为超时阈值或宿主问题后修复或放宽 | 待立 issue |

## 完成判据

计划只有在以下条件全部满足后才能标记完成：

- [ ] 所有任务卡满足 `G-*`：无 L/XL 卡、无未登记合并发布，实现写集冲突已消解（含 DEP-HF-01..04），「任务卡粒度审计表」已填齐。
- [ ] HF-01..HF-31 的 AC 全部满足，且 `Lifecycle=done` **且** `Acceptance=complete`（含 D 组 `release.yml` 证据）。
- [ ] 所有 Verification 命令已运行并记录结果；每个判据矩阵行都有对应测试与 git 2.54 对照（故障注入行与 Libra 专有兼容行按「成功定义」的例外改为源码对照或不适用），「以 git 实跑为准」的行已写回 ADR-HF-01。
- [ ] ER-HF-02、ER-HF-05、ER-HF-06 判定已记录；若拆出迁移或 diff 对齐卡，其验收已完成。
- [ ] #477 评论场景移植测试全部通过：`t3701:1452`、`:1465`、`:1479`、`:1493`、`:1525`（TC-0038..TC-0042）与 `:1446` 的 `add`/`reset`/`checkout`/`stash push` 形态；`t7105` 与选定 `t3404`/`t3437` 用例通过。
- [ ] #495 期望行为 1–4 由 M-SEQ、M-CONT、M-UNMERGED、M-LABEL 证明；隐藏参数已在 HF-19、HF-24 公开，D15、D16 已按 ADR-HF-15、ADR-HF-19 修订。
- [ ] **全量收口门（ER-13）**：`cargo +nightly fmt --all --check`、`cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`、`source .env.test && source .env.live-test && cargo nextest run --all --no-fail-fast --retries 2` 全绿（DEP-HF-05 已重核）。
- [ ] **全量暴露的 Bug 已全部处理（ER-13）**：本计划引起的失败已前滚修复并重跑全量至全绿；既有失败附计划基线 commit 上同样失败的复现证据，并登记 `FIX-*` 或 `DEFER-*`。
- [ ] docs、compat、测试索引同步完成（见「兼容与文档收口」），D-empty-message 已修订，D15/D16 已追加诊断说明。
- [ ] 每张卡的 `Rollback mode` 已验证或记录不可验证的原因。
- [ ] Codex review 最终为 `PASS`，P0/P1 全部关闭。
- [ ] 每张卡已 `patch + 1`、版本面五处一致、`Cargo.lock` 仅由工具链刷新；tag `v<VERSION>` 已在核对为已验证 `HEAD` 后推送（该 tag 推送触发 `release.yml`），`gh release create v<VERSION> -R libra-tools/libra --verify-tag` 已创建 Release 元数据，且取得 D 组逐 job 与 Cloudflare / CDN 发布证据（ER-08）。
- [ ] 「修订历史」已记录成稿后的全部规范性变更。
- [ ] DEP-HF-07：经用户确认后，已在 #477 回复逐条复核结论、各卡发布版本与评论场景证据，并在 HF-01..HF-31 全部完成后关闭 #495（#477 保持关闭；或记录用户决定不回复、不关闭）。

## 附录 A：门禁脚本

卡内「判据规范」引用的脚本门（A.1–A.3），以及各卡 D 组的发布核对门（A.4）。执行前把代码块逐字保存到 `$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue477/` 下对应文件名，在 libra 仓库根执行。退出码 0 为通过，非 0 为不达标。清单按卡追加；卡的文档落点增减时，同批修改清单、卡内「判据规范」与 AC 分子。清单短语以 `!` 开头时，表示该落点必须不再包含其后的文本（删除类文档谓词）。A.2–A.4 要求 `LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR` 为已存在、位于调用目录之外的绝对路径，全部临时文件（含 `TMPDIR`）都建在其下，不回退到系统临时目录，也不写调用方仓库；退出码 2 表示环境或前置条件不满足。

### A.1 `docs-guard.sh`（GATE-HF-DOCS）

必须在 libra 仓库根运行（`.libra/` 与 `COMPATIBILITY.md` 存在）；以 `env -i` 重新执行，`PATH` 固定为 `/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`，Git 固定为 `/usr/bin/git`。卡的清单含网站行（`@WEB@`）时，只读取 `../libra-backend`，并先校验它只含 `.git` 元数据（同时含 `.libra`，或两者皆无，即按 ER-06a 停止）、是 Git 检出根且当前分支为 `cf`（DEP-HF-06）；设置 `LIBRA_BACKEND_CHECKOUT` 即退出 2。0 为全部落点达标，1 为缺短语、残留删除文本、行锚错误或清单无该卡，2 为环境不满足。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# GATE-HF-DOCS (#477): every ER-06a landing of one card carries its required phrase.
# Run from the libra repository root: bash docs-guard.sh HF-03
# A phrase starting with '!' asserts the landing no longer contains the rest of the phrase.
# COMPATIBILITY.md row anchors are checked by the '<line>:<row prefix>' targets; the file's line count
# is not fixed here (upstream appends sections at the end); the ledger's line anchors are checked by
# `cargo test --test compat_ledger_schema`.
# A target of the form '<file>#<row prefix>' checks only the lines of <file> that start with <row prefix>
# (for example one error-code table row); the phrase must appear on such a line, or with '!' on none.
# Website rows (@WEB@) are read only from ../libra-backend, which must be a Git checkout root on
# branch cf (DEP-HF-06); LIBRA_BACKEND_CHECKOUT is rejected. A checkout carrying both .git and .libra
# metadata, or neither, stops the gate (ER-06a). Exit 0 pass, 1 not met, 2 environment.
# The gate re-executes itself under `env -i` with PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin; Git is /usr/bin/git.
if [ -z "${HF_DOCS_CLEAN:-}" ]; then
  [ -z "${LIBRA_BACKEND_CHECKOUT+x}" ] || { echo "FAIL: LIBRA_BACKEND_CHECKOUT is not accepted; website rows are read from ../libra-backend"; exit 2; }
  exec env -i HF_DOCS_CLEAN=1 PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin /bin/bash "$0" "$@"
fi
set -uo pipefail
PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin
GIT=/usr/bin/git
CARD=${1:?usage: docs-guard.sh <HF-xx>}
[ -d .libra ] && [ -f COMPATIBILITY.md ] || { echo "FAIL: run from the libra repository root"; exit 2; }
WEB=/nonexistent-website-checkout
if grep -q "^${CARD}"$'\t''@WEB@/' "$0"; then
  BACKEND=$(cd ../libra-backend 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || { echo "FAIL: ../libra-backend is missing (DEP-HF-06)"; exit 2; }
  if [ -e "$BACKEND/.git" ] && [ -e "$BACKEND/.libra" ]; then echo "FAIL: $BACKEND has both .git and .libra metadata; stop and report (ER-06a)"; exit 2; fi
  [ -e "$BACKEND/.git" ] || { echo "FAIL: $BACKEND has no .git metadata; DEP-HF-06 requires a Git checkout (a .libra-only checkout needs a plan revision first)"; exit 2; }
  [ -x "$GIT" ] || { echo "FAIL: /usr/bin/git is required to check ../libra-backend"; exit 2; }
  [ "$("$GIT" -C "$BACKEND" rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null)" = "$BACKEND" ] || { echo "FAIL: $BACKEND is not a Git checkout root (DEP-HF-06)"; exit 2; }
  [ "$("$GIT" -C "$BACKEND" symbolic-ref --short HEAD 2>/dev/null)" = cf ] || { echo "FAIL: $BACKEND is not on branch cf (DEP-HF-06)"; exit 2; }
  WEB=$BACKEND/apps/tanstack-app/content/docs/commands
  echo "ok   ../libra-backend: Git checkout root on branch cf"
fi
fail=0
n=0
while IFS=$'\t' read -r card target phrase; do
  [ "$card" = "$CARD" ] || continue
  n=$((n + 1))
  case "$target" in
    COMPATIBILITY.md:*)
      rest=${target#COMPATIBILITY.md:}
      line=${rest%%:*}
      prefix=${rest#*:}
      row=$(sed -n "${line}p" COMPATIBILITY.md)
      case "$row" in
        "$prefix"*)
          if printf '%s\n' "$row" | grep -qF -- "$phrase"; then echo "ok   COMPATIBILITY.md:$line: $phrase"; else echo "FAIL COMPATIBILITY.md:$line: missing [$phrase]"; fail=1; fi ;;
        *) echo "FAIL COMPATIBILITY.md:$line: row does not start with [$prefix]"; fail=1 ;;
      esac ;;
    *'#|'*)
      file=${target%%#|*}; file=${file/@WEB@/$WEB}
      prefix="|${target#*#|}"
      if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then echo "FAIL $file: file missing"; fail=1; continue; fi
      rows=$(awk -v p="$prefix" 'index($0, p) == 1' "$file")
      case "$phrase" in
        '!'*)
          absent=${phrase#!}
          if printf '%s\n' "$rows" | grep -qF -- "$absent"; then echo "FAIL $file row [$prefix]: still contains [$absent]"; fail=1
          else echo "ok   $file row [$prefix]: no [$absent]"; fi ;;
        *)
          if [ -n "$rows" ] && printf '%s\n' "$rows" | grep -qF -- "$phrase"; then echo "ok   $file row [$prefix]: $phrase"
          else echo "FAIL $file row [$prefix]: missing [$phrase]"; fail=1; fi ;;
      esac ;;
    *)
      file=${target/@WEB@/$WEB}
      case "$phrase" in
        '!'*)
          absent=${phrase#!}
          if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then echo "FAIL $file: file missing"; fail=1
          elif grep -qF -- "$absent" "$file"; then echo "FAIL $file: still contains [$absent]"; fail=1
          else echo "ok   $file: no [$absent]"; fi ;;
        *)
          if grep -qF -- "$phrase" "$file" 2>/dev/null; then echo "ok   $file: $phrase"; else echo "FAIL $file: missing [$phrase]"; fail=1; fi ;;
      esac ;;
  esac
done <<'MANIFEST'
HF-03	docs/commands/cherry-pick.md	refuses to start while the index has unmerged entries
HF-03	docs/commands/zh-CN/cherry-pick.md	索引存在未合并条目时拒绝开始

MANIFEST
[ "$n" -gt 0 ] || { echo "FAIL: no manifest rows for $CARD"; fail=1; }
[ "$fail" -eq 0 ] && echo "GATE-HF-DOCS $CARD: PASS" || echo "GATE-HF-DOCS $CARD: FAIL"

```

### A.2 `fixture-gate.sh`（GATE-HF-FIXTURES，HF-15）

用隔离环境下固定的 `/usr/bin/git`（必须输出 `git version 2.54.0 (Apple Git-157)`，即 2026-09-15 事实基线实测所用构建）重新生成 M-HUNK 夹具，并与检入的 `tests/data/patch-mode/` 逐字节 `diff -r` 比较：0 为一致；1 为漂移、夹具目录缺失、夹具文件未全部进入 Libra 索引、工作树相对索引有改动，或对已提交夹具的暂存修改（相对 HEAD 只允许首次入库的 `A  ` 条目）；2 为 git 输出不是该钉定构建、与检入的 `GIT_VERSION` 不一致（来源不符）、设置了覆盖、生成命令失败、release 构建缺失或过期，或环境错误。脚本以 `env -i` 重新执行，只传入固定的 `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin` 与 `LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR`（必须为已存在、位于调用目录之外的绝对路径，否则退出 2；`TMPDIR` 与全部临时文件都建在其下）；屏蔽全局与系统 Git 配置，固定 locale 与作者时间；必须在 libra 仓库根运行（`.libra/` 存在且 `Cargo.toml` 声明 `name = "libra"`），`GIT` 固定为 `/usr/bin/git`，夹具目录固定为该仓库的 `tests/data/patch-mode/`，设置 `GIT`、`FIXTURE_DIR` 或 `LIBRA_BIN` 即退出 2；每条生成用 git 命令（含 `add -p`、`reset -p`）必须成功，否则退出 2；索引与状态检查只用本检出的 `target/release/libra`（须存在、报告 `Cargo.toml` 版本、不旧于 `src/`、`sql/`、`build.rs`、`Cargo.toml`、`Cargo.lock`），并在临时目录下的 `HOME`、`LIBRA_HOME`、XDG 与两个配置库中运行；不接受参数，从不写仓库，只写临时目录，并把生成树保留在每次新建的 `$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/hf15-generated.XXXXXX/`（路径打印在输出中，不删除既有目录）。首次生成：HF-15 实现者运行本门（夹具目录缺失时退出 1），把保留的生成树复制到 `tests/data/patch-mode/` 并 `libra add`，再运行本门至 PASS，随卡提交；之后每次验收直接运行本门。

| 夹具路径 | 内容 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|
| `GIT_VERSION` | 生成所用 `git --version` 的完整输出（须为 `git version 2.54.0 (Apple Git-157)`） | 版本钉与来源记录：与运行本门时的 git 输出不一致即退出 2 |
| `<case>/diff-files.patch` | `git diff-files -p --no-color` | H1（工作树 vs 索引） |
| `<case>/diff-cached.patch` | `git diff-index --cached -p --no-color HEAD` | H1（索引 vs HEAD） |
| `<case>/split-first-hunk.txt` | `add -p` 对首个 hunk 输入 `s` 的结果行 | H4 |
| `<case>/apply-<answers>.index` | 按应答序列执行 `git add -p`（或 `reset -p`）后的 `git ls-files -s` | H2、H3、H5 |
| `reset-nothead/diff-reverse.patch` | `git diff-index -R --cached -p --no-color HEAD^` | M-RESETP R3 反向 diff 头（HF-20 复用） |

case：`two-hunks`、`split3`、`three-files`、`deletion`、`mode`、`binary`、`crlf`、`noeol`、`empty`、`reset-head`、`reset-nothead`。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# GATE-HF-FIXTURES (#477 HF-15): regenerate the M-HUNK patch-mode fixtures with Git 2.54.0 and
# compare them byte-for-byte with the committed tests/data/patch-mode/ tree.
#   bash fixture-gate.sh   # compare only: exit 0 identical, 1 not met, 2 environment or provenance error
# The gate never writes to the repository. Each run keeps its generated tree in a fresh
# $LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/hf15-generated.XXXXXX/ directory and never removes an existing one (first
# generation: copy it into tests/data/patch-mode/, `libra add` it, then rerun this gate).
# GIT_VERSION records the full `git --version` output of the generating git; a git whose output differs
# from the committed GIT_VERSION exits 2 before any comparison. GIT is fixed to /usr/bin/git, which must
# report the pinned build exactly, and every generating git command must succeed, otherwise exit 2.
# The fixture tree is fixed to tests/data/patch-mode of the libra checkout in the current directory;
# GIT, FIXTURE_DIR and LIBRA_BIN overrides exit 2. Index checks use only this checkout's
# target/release/libra, which must report the Cargo.toml version and be newer than src/, sql/, build.rs,
# Cargo.toml and Cargo.lock; it runs with HOME, LIBRA_HOME, XDG and both config databases under the
# temporary directory. Every fixture file must be in the Libra index with no worktree change, and the
# only allowed difference from HEAD is a first-time seed (`A  `); otherwise exit 1.
# LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be an existing absolute directory outside the caller directory; every
# temporary path (including TMPDIR) is created under it, with no fallback to the system temp dir.
# Tools come only from PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin. Run from the libra repository root.
if [ -z "${HF_FIXTURE_CLEAN:-}" ]; then
  [ -z "${GIT+x}" ] && [ -z "${FIXTURE_DIR+x}" ] && [ -z "${LIBRA_BIN+x}" ] \
    || { echo "FAIL: GIT, FIXTURE_DIR and LIBRA_BIN overrides are not accepted"; exit 2; }
  exec env -i HF_FIXTURE_CLEAN=1 PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin \
    LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR="${LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR:-}" \
    /bin/bash "$0" "$@"
fi
set -uo pipefail
PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin
[ "$#" -eq 0 ] || { echo "usage: $0   (compare only; takes no arguments)"; exit 2; }
GIT=/usr/bin/git
PINNED_GIT_VERSION="git version 2.54.0 (Apple Git-157)"
[ -d .libra ] && grep -qx 'name = "libra"' Cargo.toml 2>/dev/null || { echo "FAIL: run from the libra repository root"; exit 2; }
REPO=$(pwd -P) || exit 2
FIXTURE_DIR="$REPO/tests/data/patch-mode"
CARGO_VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^version = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' Cargo.toml | head -1)
[ -n "$CARGO_VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: cannot read the package version from Cargo.toml"; exit 2; }
LIBRA_BIN="$REPO/target/release/libra"
[ -x "$LIBRA_BIN" ] || { echo "FAIL: $LIBRA_BIN is missing; run LIBRA_SKIP_WEB_BUILD=1 cargo build --release first"; exit 2; }
for p in src sql build.rs Cargo.toml Cargo.lock; do
  [ -e "$p" ] || continue
  newer=$(find "$p" -newer "$LIBRA_BIN" -print 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -z "$newer" ] || { echo "FAIL: $LIBRA_BIN is older than $newer; rebuild the release binary"; exit 2; }
done
case "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" in /*) ;; *) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be an absolute path to an existing directory"; exit 2 ;; esac
SCRATCH=$(cd "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || { echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR [$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR] is not a directory"; exit 2; }
CALLER=$(pwd -P) || exit 2
case "$SCRATCH/" in "$CALLER/"*) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be outside the caller directory [$CALLER]"; exit 2 ;; esac
GIT_VER=$("$GIT" --version) || { echo "FAIL: cannot run [$GIT]"; exit 2; }
[ "$GIT_VER" = "$PINNED_GIT_VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: need [$PINNED_GIT_VERSION] (the 2026-09-15 fact-baseline build), got [$GIT_VER]"; exit 2; }
if [ -f "$FIXTURE_DIR/GIT_VERSION" ] && [ "$(cat "$FIXTURE_DIR/GIT_VERSION")" != "$GIT_VER" ]; then
  echo "FAIL: fixtures were generated by [$(cat "$FIXTURE_DIR/GIT_VERSION")], this git is [$GIT_VER]"; exit 2
fi
W=$(mktemp -d "$SCRATCH/hf15-fixtures.XXXXXX") || exit 2
[ -n "$W" ] && [ -d "$W" ] || exit 2
trap 'rm -rf -- "$W"' EXIT
mkdir -p "$W/tmp" "$W/libra-env/home" "$W/libra-env/libra-home" "$W/libra-env/xdg" || exit 2
run_libra() {
  env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin HOME="$W/libra-env/home" LIBRA_HOME="$W/libra-env/libra-home" \
    XDG_CONFIG_HOME="$W/libra-env/xdg" LIBRA_CONFIG_GLOBAL_DB="$W/libra-env/libra-home/config.db" \
    LIBRA_CONFIG_SYSTEM_DB="$W/libra-env/libra-home/system-config.db" TMPDIR="$W/tmp" \
    "$LIBRA_BIN" "$@" </dev/null
}
[ "$(run_libra --version 2>/dev/null)" = "libra $CARGO_VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: $LIBRA_BIN does not report libra $CARGO_VERSION (Cargo.toml)"; exit 2; }
export GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0 HOME="$W" TMPDIR="$W/tmp" LC_ALL=C \
  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=t GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL=t@example.com GIT_COMMITTER_NAME=t GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL=t@example.com \
  GIT_AUTHOR_DATE=2026-01-01T00:00:00Z GIT_COMMITTER_DATE=2026-01-01T00:00:00Z GIT_EDITOR=:
OUT="$W/out"
mkdir -p "$OUT" || exit 2
printf '%s\n' "$GIT_VER" > "$OUT/GIT_VERSION"

lines() { seq 1 "$1" | sed 's/^/line /'; }
new_repo() {
  rm -rf "$W/r" && "$GIT" init -q --template= -b main "$W/r" && cd "$W/r" || exit 2
  "$GIT" config core.autocrlf false && "$GIT" config core.filemode true || exit 2
}
commit_all() { "$GIT" add -A && "$GIT" commit -q -m "$1" || { echo "FAIL: git commit failed ($1)"; exit 2; }; }
record() { # case
  mkdir -p "$OUT/$1" || exit 2
  "$GIT" diff-files -p --no-color > "$OUT/$1/diff-files.patch" || { echo "FAIL: git diff-files failed ($1)"; exit 2; }
  "$GIT" diff-index --cached -p --no-color HEAD > "$OUT/$1/diff-cached.patch" || { echo "FAIL: git diff-index failed ($1)"; exit 2; }
}
split_probe() { # case  (first hunk, answers "s" then quit; decisions none)
  local out
  out=$(printf 's\nq\n' | "$GIT" add -p 2>&1) || { echo "FAIL: git add -p split probe failed ($1)"; exit 2; }
  printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -E -o 'Split into [0-9]+ hunks\.|Sorry, cannot split this hunk' | head -1 > "$OUT/$1/split-first-hunk.txt"
  [ -s "$OUT/$1/split-first-hunk.txt" ] || { echo "FAIL: git add -p printed no split result ($1)"; exit 2; }
}
apply_mask() { # case name answers [command...]
  local case=$1 name=$2 answers=$3; shift 3
  rm -rf "$W/m" && cp -R "$W/r" "$W/m" || exit 2
  ( cd "$W/m" && printf "$answers" | "$GIT" "$@" >/dev/null 2>&1 ) || { echo "FAIL: git $* failed for $case/$name"; exit 2; }
  ( cd "$W/m" && "$GIT" ls-files -s ) > "$OUT/$case/apply-$name.index" || { echo "FAIL: git ls-files failed for $case/$name"; exit 2; }
  rm -rf "$W/m"
}

# two-hunks: one file, two separate hunks (H1, H2)
new_repo; lines 20 > f.txt; commit_all base
sed -e 's/^line 2$/line 2 changed/' -e 's/^line 18$/line 18 changed/' f.txt > f.tmp && mv f.tmp f.txt || exit 2
record two-hunks
for a in yy yn ny nn; do apply_mask two-hunks "$a" "$(printf '%s' "$a" | sed 's/./&\\n/g')" add -p; done

# split3: one hunk splittable into three (P5, H4)
new_repo; lines 10 > s.txt; commit_all base
sed -e 's/^line 4$/line 4 x/' -e 's/^line 6$/line 6 x/' -e 's/^line 8$/line 8 x/' s.txt > s.tmp && mv s.tmp s.txt || exit 2
record split3; split_probe split3
for a in yny nyn yyy nnn; do apply_mask split3 "s$a" "s\n$(printf '%s' "$a" | sed 's/./&\\n/g')" add -p; done

# three-files: each file one hunk splittable into two (P6)
new_repo; for f in a b c; do lines 8 > "$f.txt"; done; commit_all base
for f in a b c; do sed -e 's/^line 3$/line 3 x/' -e 's/^line 5$/line 5 x/' "$f.txt" > t && mv t "$f.txt" || exit 2; done
record three-files; split_probe three-files
apply_mask three-files tc0042 's\ny\nn\ns\nn\ny\ns\ny\ny\n' add -p

# deletion (H3)
new_repo; lines 3 > d.txt; lines 3 > keep.txt; commit_all base; rm d.txt || exit 2
record deletion
for a in y n; do apply_mask deletion "$a" "$a\n" add -p; done

# mode change (H3)
new_repo; printf '#!/bin/sh\n' > m.sh; commit_all base; chmod +x m.sh || exit 2
record mode
for a in y n; do apply_mask mode "$a" "$a\n" add -p; done

# binary (H3: not selectable)
new_repo; printf 'a\000b\000' > bin.dat; lines 3 > t.txt; commit_all base
printf 'a\000c\000' > bin.dat; sed 's/^line 2$/line 2 x/' t.txt > t2 && mv t2 t.txt || exit 2
record binary
apply_mask binary y 'y\n' add -p

# crlf / noeol / empty (H5)
new_repo; printf 'a\r\nb\r\nc\r\n' > crlf.txt; commit_all base; printf 'a\r\nB\r\nc\r\n' > crlf.txt
record crlf; for a in y n; do apply_mask crlf "$a" "$a\n" add -p; done
new_repo; printf 'a\nb' > noeol.txt; commit_all base; printf 'a\nB' > noeol.txt
record noeol; for a in y n; do apply_mask noeol "$a" "$a\n" add -p; done
new_repo; : > e.txt; commit_all base; lines 2 > e.txt
record empty; for a in y n; do apply_mask empty "$a" "$a\n" add -p; done

# reset -p against HEAD (Unstage) and against HEAD^ (Apply, reverse diff; HF-20 M-RESETP R2/R3)
new_repo; lines 4 > foo; lines 4 > bar; commit_all base
{ sed 's/^line 1$/line 1 x/' foo > t && mv t foo && sed 's/^line 1$/line 1 x/' bar > t && mv t bar && "$GIT" add foo bar; } || exit 2
record reset-head
apply_mask reset-head ny 'n\ny\n' reset -p
new_repo; lines 4 > bar; commit_all base; sed 's/^line 2$/line 2 x/' bar > t && mv t bar || exit 2; commit_all change
mkdir -p "$OUT/reset-nothead" || exit 2
"$GIT" diff-index -R --cached -p --no-color HEAD^ > "$OUT/reset-nothead/diff-reverse.patch" || { echo "FAIL: git diff-index -R failed"; exit 2; }
apply_mask reset-nothead y 'y\n' reset -p HEAD^

cd "$W" || exit 2
# Keep the generated tree in a fresh directory inside the scratch directory; never touch the repository.
GEN=$(mktemp -d "$SCRATCH/hf15-generated.XXXXXX") || { echo "FAIL: cannot create a generated-tree directory"; exit 2; }
cp -R "$OUT/." "$GEN/" || { echo "FAIL: cannot keep generated tree at $GEN"; exit 2; }
echo "info generated tree kept at $GEN"
[ -d "$FIXTURE_DIR" ] || { echo "FAIL: fixture dir [$FIXTURE_DIR] missing (first generation: copy $GEN/ into tests/data/patch-mode/ and libra add it)"; exit 1; }
listed=$(cd "$REPO" && run_libra ls-files -- tests/data/patch-mode) || { echo "FAIL: libra ls-files failed"; exit 2; }
present=$(cd "$REPO" && find tests/data/patch-mode -type f | LC_ALL=C sort)
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$listed" | LC_ALL=C sort)" = "$present" ] || { echo "FAIL: tests/data/patch-mode has files outside the Libra index (libra add them)"; exit 1; }
porcelain=$(cd "$REPO" && run_libra status --porcelain -- tests/data/patch-mode) || { echo "FAIL: libra status failed"; exit 2; }
dirty=$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | grep -v '^$' | grep -v '^A  ')
[ -z "$dirty" ] || { echo "FAIL: tests/data/patch-mode differs from HEAD other than a first-time seed (only \"A  \" entries are allowed):"; printf '%s\n' "$dirty"; exit 1; }
if diff -r "$FIXTURE_DIR" "$OUT"; then
  echo "GATE-HF-FIXTURES: PASS"
else
  echo "GATE-HF-FIXTURES: FAIL (committed fixtures drift from [$GIT_VER])"; exit 1
fi
```

### A.3 `old-reader-gate.sh`（GATE-HF-OLD-READER，HF-28 M-TODO T9）

`OLD_LIBRA` 为 HF-28 之前的最新发布版，由门自行获取并认证，不接受调用方提供的 `OLD_LIBRA`、`OLD_LIBRA_VERSION` 或 `NEW_LIBRA`：取 `https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/stable/manifest-v1.json` 签名信封，用本仓库 `install.sh` 钉定的 Ed25519 公钥（`libra-release-1`，与 `src/internal/upgrade/trusted_keys.rs` 由单元测试保持一致）验签；验签工具只从固定绝对路径（`/opt/homebrew/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl`、`/usr/local/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl`、`/usr/bin/openssl`）中选第一个通过 Ed25519 自检的 OpenSSL（macOS 自带 LibreSSL 不支持 Ed25519），`PATH` 固定为 `/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`。签名版本必须等于运行目录（libra 仓库根）`Cargo.toml` 的版本、不低于 HF-21 卡 `Deliverables` 中 `release-receipt: v<X.Y.Z> <提交 sha>` 的版本、且不在 `revoked_versions` 中；本平台资产 URL 必须为 `https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/v<版本>/libra-<平台>`，下载后先核对大小与 sha256，再 `chmod` 并要求 `--version` 报告该版本、能读取测试仓库（`rev-parse HEAD` 一致）。`NEW_LIBRA` 固定为本检出的 `target/release/libra`（HF-28 的 release 构建，不接受覆盖），须报告同一 `Cargo.toml` 版本（本门在 C 组升版本之前执行）、不旧于 `src/`、`sql/`、`build.rs`、`Cargo.toml`、`Cargo.lock`，且与发布二进制逐字节不同。旧版本尝试前 `rebase-aux.json` 与索引必须存在且可哈希，否则退出 2。任一来源校验失败退出 2。脚本以 `env -i` 重新执行，只传入 `LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR`（要求同 A.2）与固定的 `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`，`HOME`、`LIBRA_HOME`、`XDG_CONFIG_HOME`、`TMPDIR`、`LIBRA_CONFIG_GLOBAL_DB`、`LIBRA_CONFIG_SYSTEM_DB` 全部指向其下的临时目录；仓库以 `init --vault false` 在 `$W/repo` 创建（默认 vault 会在 home 目录写密钥，`src/internal/vault.rs:916-934`；仓库目录与 `LIBRA_HOME` 分离，满足 `src/command/init.rs:550-554`），因此只写临时目录，不读写调用方的用户级状态。由 `NEW_LIBRA` 制造一个冲突停止的交互式 rebase，断言 `OLD_LIBRA rebase --continue` 非零退出，HEAD、索引与 `rebase-aux.json` 不变，随后 `NEW_LIBRA rebase --abort` 仍可收尾。`rebase-aux.json` 位于 worktree gitdir（主工作树为 `.libra/`，`src/command/rebase.rs:123`）；进行中提示文案以 `src/command/status.rs:1266` 为准，HF-28 实现时核对。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# GATE-HF-OLD-READER (#477 HF-28, M-TODO T9): an interactive rebase stopped by NEW_LIBRA must make
# OLD_LIBRA (the last published release before HF-28) fail closed: `rebase --continue` exits
# non-zero and HEAD, the index and the in-progress state stay unchanged.
# OLD_LIBRA is never taken from the caller. The gate fetches the Ed25519-signed stable manifest,
# verifies it with the public key pinned in this checkout's install.sh (libra-release-1), requires the
# signed version to equal Cargo.toml, to be at least the HF-21 release receipt recorded in
# docs/development/plan/issues/477.md and not to be revoked, then downloads the platform asset and
# checks its size and sha256 before making it executable. NEW_LIBRA is fixed to this checkout's
# target/release/libra (no override): it must report the same Cargo.toml version (the gate runs before
# the card's version bump), be newer than src/, sql/, build.rs, Cargo.toml and Cargo.lock, and differ from
# the release binary. The interactive state file and the index must exist before the old reader runs.
# Tools come only from PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin plus the first OpenSSL at a fixed absolute
# path that passes an Ed25519 self-test.
# The gate re-executes itself under `env -i`. HOME, LIBRA_HOME, XDG_CONFIG_HOME, TMPDIR and both config
# databases point inside the temporary directory, and the repository is created with
# `init --vault false`, so no user-level state of the caller is read or written.
# LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be an existing absolute directory outside the caller directory; every
# temporary path (including TMPDIR) is created under it, with no fallback to the system temp dir.
# Run from the libra repository root. Exit 0 pass, 1 not met, 2 environment or provenance error.
if [ -z "${HF_OLD_READER_CLEAN:-}" ]; then
  [ -z "${OLD_LIBRA+x}" ] && [ -z "${OLD_LIBRA_VERSION+x}" ] && [ -z "${NEW_LIBRA+x}" ] \
    || { echo "FAIL: OLD_LIBRA, OLD_LIBRA_VERSION and NEW_LIBRA are not accepted; the gate selects and authenticates both binaries"; exit 2; }
  exec env -i HF_OLD_READER_CLEAN=1 PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin \
    LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR="${LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR:-}" \
    /bin/bash "$0" "$@"
fi
set -uo pipefail
PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin
MANIFEST_URL=https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/stable/manifest-v1.json
is_version() { case "$1" in ''|*[!0-9.]*|.*|*.|*..*|*.*.*.*) return 1 ;; *.*.*) return 0 ;; *) return 1 ;; esac; }
version_ge() { # $1 >= $2 for X.Y.Z
  local a b i; IFS=. read -r -a a <<<"$1"; IFS=. read -r -a b <<<"$2"
  for i in 0 1 2; do [ "${a[$i]}" -gt "${b[$i]}" ] && return 0; [ "${a[$i]}" -lt "${b[$i]}" ] && return 1; done; return 0
}
[ -d .libra ] && grep -qx 'name = "libra"' Cargo.toml 2>/dev/null && [ -f install.sh ] && [ -f docs/development/plan/issues/477.md ] \
  || { echo "FAIL: run from the libra repository root"; exit 2; }
CARGO_VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^version = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' Cargo.toml | head -1)
is_version "$CARGO_VERSION" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read the package version from Cargo.toml"; exit 2; }
RECEIPT=$(awk '/^### Task HF-21:/{f=1;next} /^### Task /{f=0} f && /^\*\*Deliverables:\*\*/' docs/development/plan/issues/477.md \
  | grep -o 'release-receipt: v[0-9][0-9.]* [0-9a-f]\{40\}' | head -1)
RECEIPT_VERSION=${RECEIPT#release-receipt: v}; RECEIPT_VERSION=${RECEIPT_VERSION%% *}
[ -n "$RECEIPT" ] && is_version "$RECEIPT_VERSION" || { echo "FAIL: HF-21 has no release receipt (release-receipt: v<X.Y.Z> <sha>) in its Deliverables"; exit 2; }
NEW_LIBRA="$(pwd -P)/target/release/libra"
[ -x "$NEW_LIBRA" ] || { echo "FAIL: $NEW_LIBRA is missing; run LIBRA_SKIP_WEB_BUILD=1 cargo build --release first"; exit 2; }
for p in src sql build.rs Cargo.toml Cargo.lock; do
  [ -e "$p" ] || continue
  newer=$(find "$p" -newer "$NEW_LIBRA" -print 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -z "$newer" ] || { echo "FAIL: $NEW_LIBRA is older than $newer; rebuild the release binary"; exit 2; }
done
case "$(uname -s)-$(uname -m)" in
  Darwin-arm64) PLATFORM=darwin-arm64 ;;
  Linux-x86_64) PLATFORM=linux-amd64 ;;
  Linux-aarch64|Linux-arm64) PLATFORM=linux-arm64 ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: no published libra asset for $(uname -s)-$(uname -m)"; exit 2 ;;
esac
case "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" in /*) ;; *) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be an absolute path to an existing directory"; exit 2 ;; esac
SCRATCH=$(cd "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || { echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR [$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR] is not a directory"; exit 2; }
CALLER=$(pwd -P) || exit 2
case "$SCRATCH/" in "$CALLER/"*) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be outside the caller directory [$CALLER]"; exit 2 ;; esac
W=$(mktemp -d "$SCRATCH/hf28-old-reader.XXXXXX") || exit 2
[ -n "$W" ] && [ -d "$W" ] || exit 2
trap 'rm -rf -- "$W"' EXIT
mkdir -p "$W/home" "$W/libra-home" "$W/xdg" "$W/repo" "$W/tmp" "$W/bin" "$W/verify" || exit 2
sed -n '/^LIBRA_RELEASE_MANIFEST_PUBLIC_KEY_PEM="/,/^-----END PUBLIC KEY-----"$/p' install.sh \
  | sed -e 's/^LIBRA_RELEASE_MANIFEST_PUBLIC_KEY_PEM="//' -e 's/"$//' > "$W/verify/trust.pem"
grep -q '^-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----$' "$W/verify/trust.pem" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read the pinned manifest key from install.sh"; exit 2; }
export HOME="$W/home" LIBRA_HOME="$W/libra-home" XDG_CONFIG_HOME="$W/xdg" TMPDIR="$W/tmp" \
  LIBRA_CONFIG_GLOBAL_DB="$W/libra-home/config.db" LIBRA_CONFIG_SYSTEM_DB="$W/libra-home/system-config.db" \
  LIBRA_COMMITTER_NAME=t LIBRA_COMMITTER_EMAIL=t@example.com LIBRA_NO_HOOKS=1 LC_ALL=C
OPENSSL=
for candidate in /opt/homebrew/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl /usr/local/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl /usr/bin/openssl; do
  [ -x "$candidate" ] || continue
  if ( cd "$W/verify" && printf probe > probe.bin \
       && "$candidate" genpkey -algorithm ed25519 -out probe.key >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkey -in probe.key -pubout -out probe.pub >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkeyutl -sign -inkey probe.key -rawin -in probe.bin -out probe.sig >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkeyutl -verify -pubin -inkey probe.pub -rawin -in probe.bin -sigfile probe.sig >/dev/null 2>&1 ); then
    OPENSSL=$candidate; break
  fi
done
[ -n "$OPENSSL" ] || { echo "FAIL: no OpenSSL with Ed25519 support at a fixed path (install openssl@3)"; exit 2; }
V="$W/verify"
curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize 1048576 -o "$V/envelope.json" "$MANIFEST_URL" </dev/null \
  || { echo "FAIL: cannot fetch $MANIFEST_URL"; exit 2; }
tr -d ' \t\r\n' < "$V/envelope.json" > "$V/norm.json"
sed -n 's/.*"payload":"\([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]*\)".*/\1/p' "$V/norm.json" | "$OPENSSL" base64 -d -A > "$V/payload.bin" 2>/dev/null
sed -n 's/.*{"key_id":"libra-release-1","signature":"\([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]*\)"}.*/\1/p' "$V/norm.json" | "$OPENSSL" base64 -d -A > "$V/sig.bin" 2>/dev/null
[ -s "$V/payload.bin" ] && [ -s "$V/sig.bin" ] || { echo "FAIL: stable manifest has no libra-release-1 signature or payload"; exit 2; }
{ printf 'libra-upgrade-manifest-v1\0'; cat "$V/payload.bin"; } > "$V/msg.bin"
"$OPENSSL" pkeyutl -verify -pubin -inkey "$V/trust.pem" -rawin -in "$V/msg.bin" -sigfile "$V/sig.bin" >/dev/null 2>&1 \
  || { echo "FAIL: stable manifest signature does not verify against the key pinned in install.sh"; exit 2; }
STABLE=$(grep -o '^{"channel":"stable","version":"[0-9.]*"' "$V/payload.bin" | sed 's/.*"version":"//; s/"$//')
is_version "$STABLE" || { echo "FAIL: signed manifest carries no stable X.Y.Z version"; exit 2; }
[ "$STABLE" = "$CARGO_VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: signed stable release $STABLE differs from Cargo.toml $CARGO_VERSION (rebase onto the latest release or wait for it to publish)"; exit 2; }
version_ge "$STABLE" "$RECEIPT_VERSION" || { echo "FAIL: signed stable release $STABLE is older than the HF-21 receipt $RECEIPT_VERSION"; exit 2; }
if grep -o '"revoked_versions":\[[^]]*\]' "$V/payload.bin" | grep -q "\"$STABLE\""; then echo "FAIL: stable release $STABLE is revoked"; exit 2; fi
ROW=$(grep -o "{\"platform\":\"$PLATFORM\",\"url\":\"[^\"]*\",\"sha256\":\"[0-9a-f]\\{64\\}\",\"size\":[0-9]*}" "$V/payload.bin" | head -1)
URL=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"url":"\([^"]*\)".*/\1/')
SHA=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"sha256":"\([0-9a-f]*\)".*/\1/')
SIZE=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"size":\([0-9]*\)}.*/\1/')
[ "$URL" = "https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/v$STABLE/libra-$PLATFORM" ] || { echo "FAIL: signed artifact URL for $PLATFORM is missing or off-layout"; exit 2; }
case "$SIZE" in ''|*[!0-9]*) echo "FAIL: signed artifact size is invalid"; exit 2 ;; esac
[ "$SIZE" -gt 0 ] && [ "$SIZE" -le 134217728 ] || { echo "FAIL: signed artifact size $SIZE is out of bounds"; exit 2; }
OLD_LIBRA="$W/bin/libra-$STABLE"
curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize "$SIZE" -o "$OLD_LIBRA" "$URL" </dev/null \
  || { echo "FAIL: cannot download $URL"; exit 2; }
[ "$(wc -c < "$OLD_LIBRA" | tr -d ' ')" = "$SIZE" ] || { echo "FAIL: downloaded asset size differs from the signed manifest"; exit 2; }
if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then GOT=$(sha256sum "$OLD_LIBRA" | cut -d' ' -f1); else GOT=$(shasum -a 256 "$OLD_LIBRA" | cut -d' ' -f1); fi
[ "$GOT" = "$SHA" ] || { echo "FAIL: downloaded asset sha256 differs from the signed manifest"; exit 2; }
chmod +x "$OLD_LIBRA" || exit 2
[ "$("$OLD_LIBRA" --version </dev/null 2>/dev/null)" = "libra $STABLE" ] || { echo "FAIL: the verified release does not report libra $STABLE"; exit 2; }
[ "$("$NEW_LIBRA" --version </dev/null 2>/dev/null)" = "libra $CARGO_VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: NEW_LIBRA does not report libra $CARGO_VERSION (Cargo.toml)"; exit 2; }
if cmp -s "$OLD_LIBRA" "$NEW_LIBRA"; then echo "FAIL: NEW_LIBRA is byte-identical to the v$STABLE release"; exit 2; fi
echo "ok   OLD_LIBRA = signed stable v$STABLE ($PLATFORM, sha256 verified, >= HF-21 receipt v$RECEIPT_VERSION); NEW_LIBRA reports $CARGO_VERSION"
fail=0
cd "$W/repo" || exit 2
"$NEW_LIBRA" init --vault false --quiet </dev/null >/dev/null || { echo "FAIL: NEW_LIBRA init failed"; exit 2; }
printf 'base\n' > f.txt && "$NEW_LIBRA" add f.txt </dev/null >/dev/null && "$NEW_LIBRA" commit -m init --no-verify --no-gpg-sign </dev/null >/dev/null || { echo "FAIL: setup commit init"; exit 2; }
printf 'one\n' > f.txt && "$NEW_LIBRA" commit -a -m A --no-verify --no-gpg-sign </dev/null >/dev/null || { echo "FAIL: setup commit A"; exit 2; }
printf 'two\n' > f.txt && "$NEW_LIBRA" commit -a -m B --no-verify --no-gpg-sign </dev/null >/dev/null || { echo "FAIL: setup commit B"; exit 2; }
# Swap A and B: replaying B onto init conflicts on f.txt, stopping the interactive sequence.
printf '#!/bin/sh\nawk '"'"'/^pick/{l[++n]=$0; next} {r=r $0 "\\n"} END{print l[2]; print l[1]; printf "%%s", r}'"'"' "$1" > "$1.new" && mv "$1.new" "$1"\n' > "$W/swap.sh" && chmod +x "$W/swap.sh"
GIT_SEQUENCE_EDITOR="$W/swap.sh" "$NEW_LIBRA" rebase -i HEAD~2 </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1
"$NEW_LIBRA" status </dev/null 2>&1 | grep -q 'rebase in progress' || { echo "FAIL: NEW_LIBRA did not stop in an interactive rebase"; exit 2; }
head_before=$("$NEW_LIBRA" rev-parse HEAD </dev/null)
[ "$("$OLD_LIBRA" rev-parse HEAD </dev/null 2>/dev/null)" = "$head_before" ] || { echo "FAIL: OLD_LIBRA cannot read the test repository, so its refusal would prove nothing"; exit 2; }
[ -s .libra/rebase-aux.json ] && [ -s .libra/index ] || { echo "FAIL: .libra/rebase-aux.json or .libra/index is missing, so T9 has no persisted state to protect"; exit 2; }
state_before=$(shasum .libra/rebase-aux.json | cut -d' ' -f1) && [ -n "$state_before" ] || { echo "FAIL: cannot hash .libra/rebase-aux.json"; exit 2; }
index_before=$(shasum .libra/index | cut -d' ' -f1) && [ -n "$index_before" ] || { echo "FAIL: cannot hash .libra/index"; exit 2; }
"$OLD_LIBRA" rebase --continue </dev/null >"$W/old.out" 2>&1; rc=$?
if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ]; then echo "ok   OLD_LIBRA rebase --continue exits $rc"; else echo "FAIL: OLD_LIBRA rebase --continue exited 0"; fail=1; fi
[ "$("$NEW_LIBRA" rev-parse HEAD </dev/null)" = "$head_before" ] && echo "ok   HEAD unchanged" || { echo "FAIL: HEAD moved"; fail=1; }
if [ -f .libra/rebase-aux.json ] && [ "$(shasum .libra/rebase-aux.json | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$state_before" ]; then echo "ok   interactive state unchanged"; else echo "FAIL: rebase-aux.json changed or removed"; fail=1; fi
if [ -f .libra/index ] && [ "$(shasum .libra/index | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$index_before" ]; then echo "ok   index unchanged"; else echo "FAIL: index changed or removed"; fail=1; fi
"$NEW_LIBRA" rebase --abort </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "FAIL: NEW_LIBRA could not abort after the old reader"; fail=1; }
[ "$fail" -eq 0 ] && echo "GATE-HF-OLD-READER: PASS" || echo "GATE-HF-OLD-READER: FAIL"
exit "$fail"
```

### A.4 `release-cdn-gate.sh`（GATE-HF-RELEASE-CDN，D 组）

每张卡 D 组在 `release.yml` 的 `request-stable-manifest` job 成功后，在 libra 仓库根执行 `bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue477/release-cdn-gate.sh" <VERSION>`。门以 `env -i` 重新执行，`PATH` 固定为 `/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`，用本仓库 `install.sh` 钉定的 Ed25519 公钥验签 stable manifest（验签 OpenSSL 的选取同 A.3），要求签名版本等于 `<VERSION>` 且未被撤销；逐个下载 `libra-darwin-arm64`、`libra-linux-amd64`、`libra-linux-arm64`、`libra-windows-amd64`，以及与 `libra-windows-amd64` 同字节的 `libra-windows-amd64.exe`，核对 URL 布局、大小与 sha256；再核对 CDN 上 `install.sh` 的 `DEFAULT_VERSION` 与 `install.ps1` 的 `$DefaultVersion`。下载只落在 `$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR` 下新建的临时目录（退出时删除），不执行任何下载物。0 为通过；1 为缺少产物或大小、sha256、安装脚本默认版本不符；2 为环境、签名或版本不符——包括 stable 已被其它版本覆盖，此时不回退到未认证检查，该卡保持 `remote-pending` 并由用户裁决。

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# GATE-HF-RELEASE-CDN (#477 D group): authenticate the published artifacts of release v<X.Y.Z>.
#   bash release-cdn-gate.sh <X.Y.Z>   # run from the libra repository root after release.yml succeeds
# The gate fetches the Ed25519-signed stable manifest, verifies it with the public key pinned in this
# checkout's install.sh (libra-release-1), requires the signed stable version to equal <X.Y.Z> and not to
# be revoked, then downloads every platform artifact the release publishes (darwin-arm64, linux-amd64,
# linux-arm64, windows-amd64, plus the byte-identical libra-windows-amd64.exe) and checks URL layout,
# size and sha256 against the signed manifest. It also checks the default version in the CDN install.sh
# and install.ps1. A stable manifest that already names another release exits 2; there is no
# unauthenticated fallback. Nothing downloaded is executed.
# Tools come only from PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin plus the first OpenSSL at a fixed absolute path
# that passes an Ed25519 self-test. Downloads live in a fresh directory under LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR
# (an existing absolute directory outside the caller directory) and are removed on exit.
# Exit 0 pass, 1 artifact or install-script mismatch, 2 environment, signature or version error.
if [ -z "${HF_CDN_CLEAN:-}" ]; then
  exec env -i HF_CDN_CLEAN=1 PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR="${LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR:-}" \
    /bin/bash "$0" "$@"
fi
set -uo pipefail
PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin
MANIFEST_URL=https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/stable/manifest-v1.json
[ "$#" -eq 1 ] || { echo "usage: $0 <X.Y.Z>"; exit 2; }
VERSION=$1
case "$VERSION" in ''|*[!0-9.]*|.*|*.|*..*|*.*.*.*) echo "FAIL: version must be X.Y.Z"; exit 2 ;; *.*.*) ;; *) echo "FAIL: version must be X.Y.Z"; exit 2 ;; esac
[ -d .libra ] && grep -qx 'name = "libra"' Cargo.toml 2>/dev/null && [ -f install.sh ] || { echo "FAIL: run from the libra repository root"; exit 2; }
case "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" in /*) ;; *) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be an absolute path to an existing directory"; exit 2 ;; esac
SCRATCH=$(cd "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || { echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR [$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR] is not a directory"; exit 2; }
CALLER=$(pwd -P) || exit 2
case "$SCRATCH/" in "$CALLER/"*) echo "FAIL: LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR must be outside the caller directory [$CALLER]"; exit 2 ;; esac
W=$(mktemp -d "$SCRATCH/release-cdn.XXXXXX") || exit 2
[ -n "$W" ] && [ -d "$W" ] || exit 2
trap 'rm -rf -- "$W"' EXIT
export HOME="$W" TMPDIR="$W" LC_ALL=C
sed -n '/^LIBRA_RELEASE_MANIFEST_PUBLIC_KEY_PEM="/,/^-----END PUBLIC KEY-----"$/p' install.sh \
  | sed -e 's/^LIBRA_RELEASE_MANIFEST_PUBLIC_KEY_PEM="//' -e 's/"$//' > "$W/trust.pem"
grep -q '^-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----$' "$W/trust.pem" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read the pinned manifest key from install.sh"; exit 2; }
OPENSSL=
for candidate in /opt/homebrew/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl /usr/local/opt/openssl@3/bin/openssl /usr/bin/openssl; do
  [ -x "$candidate" ] || continue
  if ( cd "$W" && printf probe > probe.bin \
       && "$candidate" genpkey -algorithm ed25519 -out probe.key >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkey -in probe.key -pubout -out probe.pub >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkeyutl -sign -inkey probe.key -rawin -in probe.bin -out probe.sig >/dev/null 2>&1 \
       && "$candidate" pkeyutl -verify -pubin -inkey probe.pub -rawin -in probe.bin -sigfile probe.sig >/dev/null 2>&1 ); then
    OPENSSL=$candidate; break
  fi
done
[ -n "$OPENSSL" ] || { echo "FAIL: no OpenSSL with Ed25519 support at a fixed path (install openssl@3)"; exit 2; }
curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize 1048576 -o "$W/envelope.json" "$MANIFEST_URL" </dev/null \
  || { echo "FAIL: cannot fetch $MANIFEST_URL"; exit 2; }
tr -d ' \t\r\n' < "$W/envelope.json" > "$W/norm.json"
sed -n 's/.*"payload":"\([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]*\)".*/\1/p' "$W/norm.json" | "$OPENSSL" base64 -d -A > "$W/payload.bin" 2>/dev/null
sed -n 's/.*{"key_id":"libra-release-1","signature":"\([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]*\)"}.*/\1/p' "$W/norm.json" | "$OPENSSL" base64 -d -A > "$W/sig.bin" 2>/dev/null
[ -s "$W/payload.bin" ] && [ -s "$W/sig.bin" ] || { echo "FAIL: stable manifest has no libra-release-1 signature or payload"; exit 2; }
{ printf 'libra-upgrade-manifest-v1\0'; cat "$W/payload.bin"; } > "$W/msg.bin"
"$OPENSSL" pkeyutl -verify -pubin -inkey "$W/trust.pem" -rawin -in "$W/msg.bin" -sigfile "$W/sig.bin" >/dev/null 2>&1 \
  || { echo "FAIL: stable manifest signature does not verify against the key pinned in install.sh"; exit 2; }
STABLE=$(grep -o '^{"channel":"stable","version":"[0-9.]*"' "$W/payload.bin" | sed 's/.*"version":"//; s/"$//')
[ "$STABLE" = "$VERSION" ] || { echo "FAIL: the signed stable manifest names v${STABLE:-?}, not v$VERSION (superseded or not yet published; no unauthenticated fallback)"; exit 2; }
if grep -o '"revoked_versions":\[[^]]*\]' "$W/payload.bin" | grep -q "\"$VERSION\""; then echo "FAIL: v$VERSION is revoked"; exit 2; fi
echo "ok   signed stable manifest names v$VERSION (libra-release-1)"
sha256_file() { if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; else shasum -a 256 "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; fi; }
fail=0
WIN_SHA= WIN_SIZE=
for PLATFORM in darwin-arm64 linux-amd64 linux-arm64 windows-amd64; do
  ROW=$(grep -o "{\"platform\":\"$PLATFORM\",\"url\":\"[^\"]*\",\"sha256\":\"[0-9a-f]\\{64\\}\",\"size\":[0-9]*}" "$W/payload.bin" | head -1)
  URL=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"url":"\([^"]*\)".*/\1/')
  SHA=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"sha256":"\([0-9a-f]*\)".*/\1/')
  SIZE=$(printf '%s' "$ROW" | sed 's/.*"size":\([0-9]*\)}.*/\1/')
  if [ -z "$ROW" ] || [ "$URL" != "https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/v$VERSION/libra-$PLATFORM" ]; then
    echo "FAIL $PLATFORM: missing or off-layout in the signed manifest"; fail=1; continue
  fi
  case "$SIZE" in ''|*[!0-9]*) echo "FAIL $PLATFORM: invalid signed size"; fail=1; continue ;; esac
  if ! curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize "$SIZE" -o "$W/libra-$PLATFORM" "$URL" </dev/null; then
    echo "FAIL $PLATFORM: cannot download $URL"; fail=1; continue
  fi
  if [ "$(wc -c < "$W/libra-$PLATFORM" | tr -d ' ')" != "$SIZE" ] || [ "$(sha256_file "$W/libra-$PLATFORM")" != "$SHA" ]; then
    echo "FAIL $PLATFORM: size or sha256 differs from the signed manifest"; fail=1
  else
    echo "ok   $PLATFORM: size $SIZE and sha256 match the signed manifest"
  fi
  [ "$PLATFORM" = windows-amd64 ] && { WIN_SHA=$SHA; WIN_SIZE=$SIZE; }
  rm -f -- "$W/libra-$PLATFORM"
done
if [ -n "$WIN_SHA" ]; then
  EXE_URL="https://download.libra.tools/libra/releases/v$VERSION/libra-windows-amd64.exe"
  if curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize "$WIN_SIZE" -o "$W/libra-windows-amd64.exe" "$EXE_URL" </dev/null \
     && [ "$(sha256_file "$W/libra-windows-amd64.exe")" = "$WIN_SHA" ]; then
    echo "ok   windows-amd64.exe: byte-identical to the signed windows-amd64 artifact"
  else
    echo "FAIL windows-amd64.exe: missing or differs from the signed windows-amd64 artifact"; fail=1
  fi
  rm -f -- "$W/libra-windows-amd64.exe"
fi
if curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize 1048576 -o "$W/install.sh" https://download.libra.tools/install.sh </dev/null \
   && grep -qx "DEFAULT_VERSION=\"v$VERSION\"" "$W/install.sh"; then
  echo "ok   CDN install.sh defaults to v$VERSION"
else
  echo "FAIL CDN install.sh does not default to v$VERSION"; fail=1
fi
if curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsS --max-redirs 0 --max-filesize 1048576 -o "$W/install.ps1" https://download.libra.tools/install.ps1 </dev/null \
   && grep -qF "\$DefaultVersion = \"v$VERSION\"" "$W/install.ps1"; then
  echo "ok   CDN install.ps1 defaults to v$VERSION"
else
  echo "FAIL CDN install.ps1 does not default to v$VERSION"; fail=1
fi
[ "$fail" -eq 0 ] && echo "GATE-HF-RELEASE-CDN v$VERSION: PASS" || echo "GATE-HF-RELEASE-CDN v$VERSION: FAIL"
exit "$fail"
```
